-- 创建目标表
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F`;
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F`(
	`fisal_year` string COMMENT '年度',
	`comp_code` string COMMENT '公司代码',
	`cust_code` string COMMENT '客户编码',
	`cust_name` string COMMENT '客户全称',
	`sales_group_master_file` string COMMENT '销售组-主档',
	`wbs` string COMMENT 'WBS',
	`wbs_sales_group` string COMMENT 'WBS-销售组',
	`special_general_ledger` string COMMENT '特别总账',
	`general_ledger_account` string COMMENT '总账科目',
	`currency` string COMMENT '货币',
	`amt` decimal(38,2) COMMENT '金额',
	`due_date` timestamp COMMENT '到期日',
	`due_days` decimal(38,2) COMMENT '到期天数',
	`n_overdue_due_days` decimal(38,2) COMMENT '未逾期到期天数',
	`so_group_code` string COMMENT '订单组编码',
	`VOUCHER_DATE`     String  COMMENT '凭证日期'       
	            )
COMMENT '应收账龄明细-区分2023年前后格式中间表'
;

-- 将数据插入中间表 DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F
WITH AGING AS (
SELECT
	T.YEAR							AS YEAR							-- 年度
	,T.COMP_CODE					AS COMP_CODE					-- 公司代码                	
	,T.CUST_CODE                    AS CUST_CODE                    -- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME                    AS CUST_NAME                    -- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE      AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE      -- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS                          AS WBS                          -- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP              AS WBS_SALES_GROUP              -- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER       AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER       -- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT       AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT       -- 总账科目
	,T.CUR_CURRENCY                 AS CURRENCY                     -- 货币
	,T.CUR_CU_CLOS_BALANCE_AMT      AS AMT                          -- 金额
	,T.DUE_DATE                     AS DUE_DATE                     -- 到期日
	,to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN ('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END,'yyyy-MM-dd')))
                                    AS DATE1						-- 查询日期
	,T.VOUCHER_DATE					AS VOUCHER_DATE					-- 凭证日期
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE                AS SO_GROUP_CODE                -- 订单组编码
	,row_number ()over(partition by t.COMP_CODE,t.CUST_CODE,t.WBS,t.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER,t.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT,t.CUR_CURRENCY,t.SO_GROUP_CODE 
	 order by t.DW_LAST_UPDATE_DATE desc) as rn
FROM 
	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_CUST_DETAIL_F T
WHERE T.POSTING_DATE <= to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END,'yyyy-MM-dd')))
AND T.CLEAR_DATE > to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END,'yyyy-MM-dd')))
AND (T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER IN('','U','Q','M','&') OR T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER IS NULL)
)
-- 查询数据落到中间表
INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F
SELECT
	T1.YEAR							AS YEAR							-- 年度
	,T1.COMP_CODE					AS COMP_CODE					-- 公司代码                	
	,T1.CUST_CODE                   AS CUST_CODE                   	-- 客户编码
	,T2.CUST_NAME                   AS CUST_NAME                   	-- 客户全称
	,T2.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE     AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE     	-- 销售组-主档
	,T1.WBS                         AS WBS                         	-- WBS
	,T2.WBS_SALES_GROUP             AS WBS_SALES_GROUP             	-- WBS-销售组
	,T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER      AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER      	-- 特别总账
	,T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT      AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT      	-- 总账科目
	,T1.CURRENCY                	AS CURRENCY                    	-- 货币
	,T1.AMT     					AS AMT                         	-- 金额
	,T1.DUE_DATE                    AS DUE_DATE                    	-- 到期日
	,CASE WHEN day(T1.DATE1 - T1.DUE_DATE) >= 0 THEN day(T1.DATE1 - T1.DUE_DATE)
		ELSE 0
	END 							AS DUE_DAYS						-- 到期天数
	,CASE WHEN day(T1.DUE_DATE - T1.DATE1) >= 0 THEN
			CASE WHEN day(T1.DATE1 - T1.VOUCHER_DATE) >= 0 THEN day(T1.DATE1 - T1.VOUCHER_DATE)
				ELSE 0
			END 
		ELSE -1
	END 							AS N_OVERDUE_DUE_DAYS			-- 未逾期到期天数
	,T1.SO_GROUP_CODE				AS SO_GROUP_CODE				-- 订单组编码
	,T1.VOUCHER_DATE                AS VOUCHER_DATE                --凭证日期（10月12日新增）
FROM 
	AGING T1
join AGING t2 on T1.COMP_CODE   = T2.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T2.CUST_CODE
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')			= NVL(T2.WBS,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T2.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T2.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT
AND NVL(T1.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T2.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')
and NVL(T1.CURRENCY,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T2.CURRENCY,'SNULL')
and t2.rn=1
;

-- 创建目标

-- 取本币累计余额、回款金额、逾期应收、1-90天逾期应收、91-275天逾期应收、276天-2年逾期应收、2-4年逾期应收、4年以上逾期应收
WITH ACDOCA4 AS(
SELECT
	T1.COMP_CODE		    			            			AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T1.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T1.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                			AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T1.WBS							                			AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        			AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        			AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        			AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T1.CURRENCY		    				        			AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T1.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,NVL(T1.LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM,0)           			AS LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM           		-- 本币累计余额
	,NVL(T2.LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AGO,0)      		AS LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AGO  		-- 2023年前本币累计余额
	,NVL(T3.LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AFTER,0)    		AS LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年新增本币累计余额
	,NVL(T4.REPAY_AMT,0)	            						AS REPAY_AMT             						-- 回款金额
	,CASE WHEN NVL(T5.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES,0) + NVL(T4.REPAY_AMT,0) >= 0 THEN NVL(T5.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES,0) + NVL(T4.REPAY_AMT,0)
		ELSE 0 
	END 														AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES           				-- 逾期应收
	/*,CASE WHEN NVL(T6.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_2023_AGO,0) + NVL(T4.REPAY_AMT,0) >= 0 THEN NVL(T6.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_2023_AGO,0) + NVL(T4.REPAY_AMT,0)
		ELSE 0 
		END 													AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_2023_AGO           		-- 2023年前逾期应收	*/
	,NVL(T7.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AGO,0) + NVL(T8.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AGO,0) + NVL(T9.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AGO,0)
																AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_90_DAY_2023_AGO    	-- 2023年前1-90天逾期应收
	,NVL(T7.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AGO,0)			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AGO    	-- 2023年前1-30天逾期应收
	,NVL(T8.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AGO,0)			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AGO   	-- 2023年前31-60天逾期应收
	,NVL(T9.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AGO,0)			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前61-90天逾期应收
	,NVL(T10.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AGO,0) + NVL(T11.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AGO,0) + NVL(T12.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AGO,0) + NVL(T13.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AGO,0) + NVL(T14.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AGO,0) + NVL(T15.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AGO,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_275_DAY_2023_AGO 		-- 2023年前91-275天逾期应收
	,NVL(T10.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AGO,0)			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AGO 		-- 2023年前91-120天逾期应收
	,NVL(T11.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AGO,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前121-150天逾期应收
	,NVL(T12.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AGO,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前151-180天逾期应收
	,NVL(T13.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AGO,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前181-210天逾期应收
	,NVL(T14.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AGO,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前211-240天逾期应收
	,NVL(T15.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AGO,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前241-275天逾期应收
	,NVL(T16.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AGO,0) + NVL(T17.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO,0)
																AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_730_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前276天-2年逾期应收
	,NVL(T16.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AGO,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前276天-1年逾期应收
	,NVL(T17.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO 	-- 2023年前1-2年逾期应收
	,NVL(T18.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO,0) + NVL(T19.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO,0)
																AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1460_DAY_2023_AGO	-- 2023年前2-4年逾期应收
	,NVL(T18.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO	-- 2023年前2-3年逾期应收
	,NVL(T19.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO	-- 2023年前3-4年逾期应收
	,NVL(T20.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO,0)			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前4年以上逾期应收
	/*,CASE WHEN NVL(T21.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_2023_AFTER,0) + NVL(T4.REPAY_AMT,0) >= 0 THEN NVL(T21.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_2023_AFTER,0) + NVL(T4.REPAY_AMT,0)
		ELSE 0 
		END 													AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_2023_AFTER           	-- 2023年后新增逾期应收 */
	,NVL(T22.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AFTER,0) + NVL(T23.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AFTER,0) + NVL(T24.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)					AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_90_DAY_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年后1-90天逾期应收
	,NVL(T22.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年后1-30天逾期应收
	,NVL(T23.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年后31-60天逾期应收
	,NVL(T24.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年后61-90天逾期应收
	,NVL(T25.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AFTER,0) + NVL(T26.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AFTER,0) + NVL(T27.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AFTER,0) + NVL(T28.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AFTER,0) + NVL(T29.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AFTER,0) + NVL(T30.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)
																AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_275_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后91-275天逾期应收
	,NVL(T25.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后91-120天逾期应收
	,NVL(T26.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后121-150天逾期应收
	,NVL(T27.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后151-180天逾期应收
	,NVL(T28.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后181-210天逾期应收
	,NVL(T29.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后211-240天逾期应收
	,NVL(T30.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后241-275天逾期应收
	,NVL(T31.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AFTER,0) + NVL(T32.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)
																AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_730_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后276天-2年逾期应收
	,NVL(T31.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后276天-1年逾期应收
	,NVL(T32.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后1-2年逾期应收
	,NVL(T33.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER,0) + NVL(T34.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)
																AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后2-4年逾期应收
	,NVL(T33.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后2-3年逾期应收
	,NVL(T34.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)	AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后3-4年逾期应收
	,NVL(T35.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER,0)			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年后4年以上逾期应收
	,NVL(T36.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY,0) + NVL(T37.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY,0) + NVL(T38.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY,0) + NVL(T39.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY,0) + NVL(T40.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY,0) + NVL(T41.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY,0) + NVL(T42.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY,0) + NVL(T43.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY,0) + NVL(T44.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY,0) + NVL(T45.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY,0) + NVL(T46.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY,0) + NVL(T47.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY,0) 
																AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_365_DAY				-- 1年内未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T36.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY,0)					AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY				-- 0-30天未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T37.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY,0)					AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY				-- 31-60天未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T38.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY,0)					AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY				-- 61-90天未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T39.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY,0)				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY				-- 91-120天未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T40.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY,0)				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY			-- 121-150天未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T41.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY,0)				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY			-- 151-180天未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T42.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY,0)				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY			-- 181-210天未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T43.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY,0)				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY			-- 211-240天未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T44.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY,0)				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY			-- 241-270天未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T45.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY,0)				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY			-- 271-300天未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T46.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY,0)				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY			-- 301-330天未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T47.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY,0)				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY			-- 331-365天未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T48.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY,0)				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY			-- 1-2年未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T49.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY,0)				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY			-- 2-3年未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T50.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY,0)				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY			-- 3-4年未逾期应收余额
	,NVL(T51.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY,0)					AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY				-- 4年以上未逾期应收余额
	,greatest(T52.before_year_receivables,0)                    AS before_year_receivables                      --查询年前应收
FROM
-- 取本币累计余额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，过账日期（ACDOCA-BUDAT）小于等于‘查询日期’的本币金额汇总
(SELECT
	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                			AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS							                			AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        			AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        			AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        			AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        				AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,SUM(NVL(T.AMT,0)) 											AS LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM    				-- 本币累计余额
FROM 
	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F T
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T1

LEFT JOIN 
-- 2023年前本币累计余额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，过账日期（ACDOCA-BUDAT）小于等于‘查询日期’，而且年度（ACDOCA-GJAHR)小于“入参年度”的本币金额汇总
--修改于10月12日：年度（ACDOCA-GJAHR)改为凭证日期VOUCHER_DATE的年度
(
SELECT 
	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                			AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS							                			AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        			AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        			AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        			AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        				AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,SUM(NVL(T.AMT,0)) 											AS LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AGO          -- 2023年前本币累计余额
FROM 
	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F T
WHERE --T.FISAL_YEAR < '${PERIOD_ID_Y}' （改前）
SUBSTR(T.VOUCHER_DATE,1,4) <'${PERIOD_ID_Y}' 
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T2
ON 
T1.COMP_CODE                    = T2.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T2.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T2.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T2.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T2.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T2.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T2.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T2.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT
AND NVL(T1.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T2.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')

LEFT JOIN 
-- 2023年新增本币累计余额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，过账日期（ACDOCA-BUDAT）小于等于‘查询日期’，而且年度（ACDOCA-GJAHR)大于等于“入参年度”的本币金额汇总
--修改于10月12日：年度（ACDOCA-GJAHR)改为凭证日期VOUCHER_DATE的年度
(
SELECT 
	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                			AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS							                			AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        			AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        			AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        			AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        				AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,SUM(NVL(T.AMT,0)) 											AS LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AFTER        -- 2023年新增本币累计余额
FROM 
	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F T
WHERE 
--T.FISAL_YEAR >= '${PERIOD_ID_Y}'   改前
SUBSTR(T.VOUCHER_DATE,1,4) >='${PERIOD_ID_Y}'   --10月12日改后

GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T3
ON 
T1.COMP_CODE                    = T3.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T3.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T3.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T3.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T3.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T3.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T3.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T3.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT
AND NVL(T1.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T3.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')

LEFT JOIN 
-- 回款金额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，金额小于0的本币金额汇总
(
SELECT 
	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                			AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS							                			AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        			AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        			AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        			AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        				AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,SUM(NVL(T.AMT,0)) 											AS REPAY_AMT         							-- 回款金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F T
WHERE T.AMT < 0
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T4
ON 
T1.COMP_CODE                    = T4.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T4.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T4.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T4.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T4.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T4.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T4.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T4.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT
AND NVL(T1.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T4.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')

LEFT JOIN 
-- 逾期应收：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，金额大于0，而且“到期日”<“查询日期”的本币金额汇总,汇总金额与“回款金额”合计，当小于0时，值为0；
(
SELECT 
 	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                			AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS							                			AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        			AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        			AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        			AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        				AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,SUM(NVL(T.AMT,0)) 											AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES         					-- 逾期应收
FROM 
	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F  T
WHERE T.AMT > 0 AND T.DUE_DATE < to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END,'yyyy-MM-dd')))
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T5
ON 
T1.COMP_CODE                    = T5.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T5.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T5.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T5.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T5.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T5.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T5.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T5.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT
AND NVL(T1.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T5.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')

LEFT JOIN 
-- 取2023年前1-30天逾期应收余额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，金额大于0，“到期日”<“查询日期”，年度（ACDOCA-GJAHR)小于“入参年度”，且到期天数1-30天的本币金额汇总
--修改于10月12日：年度（ACDOCA-GJAHR)改为凭证日期VOUCHER_DATE的年度
(
SELECT 
 	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                			AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS							                			AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        			AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        			AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        			AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        				AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,SUM(NVL(T.AMT,0)) AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AGO         -- 2023年前1-30天逾期应收
FROM 
	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F T
WHERE T.AMT > 0 AND T.DUE_DATE < to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END,'yyyy-MM-dd'))) 

--AND T.FISAL_YEAR < '${PERIOD_ID_Y}'  改前
AND SUBSTR(T.VOUCHER_DATE,1,4) < '${PERIOD_ID_Y}'   

AND 1 <= T.DUE_DAYS AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 30
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T7
ON 
T1.COMP_CODE                    = T7.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T7.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T7.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T7.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T7.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T7.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T7.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T7.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT
AND NVL(T1.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T7.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')

LEFT JOIN 
-- 取2023年前31-60天逾期应收余额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，金额大于0，“到期日”<“查询日期”，年度（ACDOCA-GJAHR)小于“入参年度”，且到期天数31-60天的本币金额汇总
--修改于10月12日：年度（ACDOCA-GJAHR)改为凭证日期VOUCHER_DATE的年度

 	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码

ON

AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T8.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T8.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T8.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T8.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T8.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T8.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER

AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T9.WBS,'SNULL')

-- 取2023年前4年以上逾期应收余额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，金额大于0，“到期日”<“查询日期”，年度（ACDOCA-GJAHR)小于“入参年度”，且到期天数1461天及以上的本币金额汇总
--修改于10月12日：年度（ACDOCA-GJAHR)改为凭证日期VOUCHER_DATE的年度
(
SELECT 
 	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                			AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS							                			AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        			AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        			AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        			AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        				AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,SUM(NVL(T.AMT,0)) 											AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO        -- 2023年前4年以上逾期应收
FROM 
	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F  T
WHERE T.AMT > 0 AND T.DUE_DATE < to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END,'yyyy-MM-dd'))) 

--AND T.FISAL_YEAR < '${PERIOD_ID_Y}' 
AND SUBSTR(T.VOUCHER_DATE,1,4) < '${PERIOD_ID_Y}' 

AND 1461 <= T.DUE_DAYS
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T20
ON 
T1.COMP_CODE                    = T20.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T20.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T20.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T20.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T20.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T20.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T20.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER

WHERE T.AMT > 0 AND T.DUE_DATE < to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END,'yyyy-MM-dd'))) 

--AND T.FISAL_YEAR >= '${PERIOD_ID_Y}' 
AND SUBSTR(T.VOUCHER_DATE,1,4) >= '${PERIOD_ID_Y}'

AND 1096 <= T.DUE_DAYS AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1460
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T34
ON 
T1.COMP_CODE                    = T34.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T34.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T34.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T34.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T34.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T34.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T34.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T34.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT
AND NVL(T1.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T34.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')

LEFT JOIN 
-- 取2023年后4年以上逾期应收余额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，金额大于0，“到期日”<“查询日期”，年度（ACDOCA-GJAHR)大于等于“入参年度”，且到期天数1461天及以上的本币金额汇总
--修改于10月12日：年度（ACDOCA-GJAHR)改为凭证日期VOUCHER_DATE的年度
(
SELECT 
 	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                			AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS							                			AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        			AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        			AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        			AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        				AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,SUM(NVL(T.AMT,0)) 											AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER      -- 2023年后4年以上逾期应收
FROM 
	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F  T
WHERE T.AMT > 0 AND T.DUE_DATE < to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END,'yyyy-MM-dd'))) 

--AND T.FISAL_YEAR >= '${PERIOD_ID_Y}' 
AND SUBSTR(T.VOUCHER_DATE,1,4) >= '${PERIOD_ID_Y}'

AND 1461 <= T.DUE_DAYS
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T35
ON 
T1.COMP_CODE                    = T35.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T35.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T35.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T35.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T35.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T35.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T35.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T35.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT
AND NVL(T1.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T35.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')

LEFT JOIN 
-- 取0-30天未逾期应收余额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，金额大于0，“到期日”>=“查询日期”，且到期天数0-30天的本币金额汇总
(
SELECT 
 	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                			AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS							                			AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        			AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        			AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        			AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        				AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,SUM(NVL(T.AMT,0)) 											AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY         		-- 0-30天未逾期应收余额
FROM 
	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F  T
WHERE T.AMT > 0 AND T.DUE_DATE >= to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END,'yyyy-MM-dd'))) AND 0 <= T.N_OVERDUE_DUE_DAYS AND T.N_OVERDUE_DUE_DAYS <= 30
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T36
ON 
T1.COMP_CODE                    = T36.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T36.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T36.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T36.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T36.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T36.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T36.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T36.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT
AND NVL(T1.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T36.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')

LEFT JOIN 
-- 取31-60天未逾期应收余额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，金额大于0，“到期日”>=“查询日期”，且到期天数31-60天的本币金额汇总
(
SELECT 
 	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                			AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档

	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        			AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        			AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        			AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        				AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,SUM(NVL(T.AMT,0)) 											AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY         		-- 31-60天未逾期应收余额
FROM 
	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F  T
WHERE T.AMT > 0 AND T.DUE_DATE >= to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END,'yyyy-MM-dd'))) AND 31 <= T.N_OVERDUE_DUE_DAYS AND T.N_OVERDUE_DUE_DAYS <= 60
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE

) T37
ON 
T1.COMP_CODE                    = T37.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T37.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T37.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T37.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T37.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T37.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T37.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T37.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT
AND NVL(T1.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T37.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')

LEFT JOIN 
-- 取61-90天未逾期应收余额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，金额大于0，“到期日”>=“查询日期”，且到期天数61-90天的本币金额汇总
(
SELECT 
 	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                			AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS							                			AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        			AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        			AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        			AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        				AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,SUM(NVL(T.AMT,0)) 											AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY         		-- 61-90天未逾期应收余额
FROM 
	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F  T
WHERE T.AMT > 0 AND T.DUE_DATE >= to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END,'yyyy-MM-dd'))) AND 61 <= T.N_OVERDUE_DUE_DAYS AND T.N_OVERDUE_DUE_DAYS <= 90
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T38
ON 
T1.COMP_CODE                    = T38.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T38.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T38.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T38.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T38.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T38.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T38.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T38.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT
AND NVL(T1.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T38.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')

LEFT JOIN 
-- 取91-120天未逾期应收余额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，金额大于0，“到期日”>=“查询日期”，且到期天数91-120天的本币金额汇总
(

 	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称

	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,SUM(NVL(T.AMT,0)) 											AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY         	-- 91-120天未逾期应收余额

	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F  T
WHERE T.AMT > 0 AND T.DUE_DATE >= to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END,'yyyy-MM-dd'))) AND 91 <= T.N_OVERDUE_DUE_DAYS AND T.N_OVERDUE_DUE_DAYS <= 120
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T39
ON

AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T39.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T39.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T39.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T39.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T39.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T39.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T39.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT

LEFT JOIN 
-- 取121-150天未逾期应收余额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，金额大于0，“到期日”>=“查询日期”，且到期天数121-150天的本币金额汇总

LEFT JOIN 
-- 取151-180天未逾期应收余额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，金额大于0，“到期日”>=“查询日期”，且到期天数151-180天的本币金额汇总

) T41
ON

AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T41.CUST_CODE

	,T.CURRENCY		    				        				AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,SUM(NVL(T.AMT,0)) 											AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY         	-- 2-3年未逾期应收余额
FROM 
	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F  T
WHERE T.AMT > 0 AND T.DUE_DATE >= to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END,'yyyy-MM-dd'))) AND 731 <= T.N_OVERDUE_DUE_DAYS AND T.N_OVERDUE_DUE_DAYS <= 1095
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T49
ON 
T1.COMP_CODE                    = T49.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T49.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T49.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T49.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T49.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T49.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T49.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T49.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT
AND NVL(T1.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T49.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')

LEFT JOIN 
-- 取3-4年未逾期应收余额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，金额大于0，“到期日”>=“查询日期”，且到期天数1096-1460天的本币金额汇总
(
SELECT 
 	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                			AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS							                			AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        			AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        			AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        			AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        				AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,SUM(NVL(T.AMT,0)) 											AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY         	-- 3-4年未逾期应收余额
FROM 
	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F  T
WHERE T.AMT > 0 AND T.DUE_DATE >= to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END,'yyyy-MM-dd'))) AND 1096 <= T.N_OVERDUE_DUE_DAYS AND T.N_OVERDUE_DUE_DAYS <= 1460
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T50
ON 
T1.COMP_CODE                    = T50.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T50.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T50.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T50.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T50.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T50.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T50.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T50.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT
AND NVL(T1.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T50.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')

LEFT JOIN 
-- 取4年以上未逾期应收余额：按公司代码、客户编码、销售组-主档、WBS、特别总账、总账科目维度，金额大于0，“到期日”>=“查询日期”，且到期天数1461天及以上的本币金额汇总
(
SELECT 
 	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                			AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS							                			AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        			AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        			AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        			AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        				AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,SUM(NVL(T.AMT,0)) 											AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY         		-- 4年以上未逾期应收余额
FROM 
	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F  T
WHERE T.AMT > 0 AND T.DUE_DATE >= to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END,'yyyy-MM-dd'))) AND 1461 <= T.N_OVERDUE_DUE_DAYS 
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T51
ON 
T1.COMP_CODE                    = T51.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T51.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T51.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T51.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T51.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T51.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T51.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T51.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT
AND NVL(T1.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T51.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')

LEFT JOIN 
-- 【按公司代码、客户编码、WBS、总账科目、订单组维度，过账日期（ACDOCA-BUDAT）小于等于‘查询日期’，而且年度（ACDOCA-GJAHR)小于“入参年度”，而且金额小于0的本币金额合计】
-- 【按公司代码、客户编码、WBS、总账科目、订单组维度，过账日期（ACDOCA-BUDAT）小于等于‘查询日期’，而且金额大于0的本币金额合计】
(
SELECT 
	T.COMP_CODE		    			            				AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        			AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            			AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                			AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS							                			AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        			AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        			AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        			AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        				AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE											AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	--查询年前应收逻辑变更10月14日：年度改为过账年度
	,SUM(CASE WHEN 
	--T.FISAL_YEAR < '${PERIOD_ID_Y}' 
	SUBSTR(T.VOUCHER_DATE,1,4) < '${PERIOD_ID_Y}'
	AND T.AMT>0 THEN NVL(T.AMT,0) ELSE 0 END)+
	 SUM(CASE WHEN T.AMT<0 THEN NVL(T.AMT,0) ELSE 0 END) AS before_year_receivables          -- 查询年前应收
FROM 
	DWRFIN.DWR_AR_M3_F  T
GROUP BY T.COMP_CODE ,T.CUST_CODE ,T.CUST_NAME ,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE ,T.WBS ,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT ,T.CURRENCY,T.SO_GROUP_CODE
) T52
ON 
T1.COMP_CODE                    = T52.COMP_CODE		    
AND T1.CUST_CODE		        = T52.CUST_CODE
AND T1.CUST_NAME			    = T52.CUST_NAME
AND NVL(T1.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')  = NVL(T52.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS,'SNULL')					    = NVL(T52.WBS,'SNULL')
AND NVL(T1.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T52.WBS_SALES_GROUP,'SNULL')
AND T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER   = T52.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER
AND T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT   = T52.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT
AND NVL(T1.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')		    = NVL(T52.SO_GROUP_CODE,'SNULL')

)
,
ACDOCA5 AS(
SELECT
 	T.COMP_CODE		    			            		AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        	AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            	AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                	AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS							                	AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        	AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        	AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        	AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        		AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM           			AS LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM           		-- 本币累计余额
	,T.LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AGO         		AS LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AGO  		-- 2023年前本币累计余额
	,T.LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AFTER       		AS LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年新增本币累计余额
	,T.REPAY_AMT	            						AS REPAY_AMT             						-- 回款金额
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES								AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES           				-- 逾期应收
	/*,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_2023_AGO						AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_2023_AGO           		-- 2023年前逾期应收	*/
	,CASE WHEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.REPAY_AMT >= 0 THEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.REPAY_AMT
		ELSE 0
	END 												AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO	    -- 2023年前4年以上逾期应收
	,CASE WHEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.REPAY_AMT >= 0 THEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.REPAY_AMT >= 0 THEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.REPAY_AMT
				ELSE 0
			END
	END 												AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER	    -- 2023年后4年以上逾期应收
	
	,CASE WHEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.REPAY_AMT >= 0 THEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.REPAY_AMT >= 0 THEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.REPAY_AMT
				ELSE 0
			END
	END 												AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO	-- 2023年前3-4年逾期应收
	
	,CASE WHEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.REPAY_AMT >= 0 THEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.REPAY_AMT >= 0 THEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.REPAY_AMT
				ELSE 0
			END
	END 												AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后3-4年逾期应收 
	
	,CASE WHEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.REPAY_AMT >= 0 THEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO + T.REPAY_AMT >= 0 THEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO + T.REPAY_AMT
				ELSE 0
			END
	END 												AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO	-- 2023年前2-3年逾期应收
	
	,CASE WHEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO + T.REPAY_AMT >= 0 THEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.REPAY_AMT >= 0 THEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.REPAY_AMT
				ELSE 0
			END
	END 												AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后2-3年逾期应收
	
	,CASE WHEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.REPAY_AMT >= 0 THEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO + T.REPAY_AMT >= 0 THEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO + T.REPAY_AMT
				ELSE 0
			END
	END 												AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前1-2年逾期应收
	
	,CASE WHEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO + T.REPAY_AMT >= 0 THEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AFTER
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.REPAY_AMT >= 0 THEN T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.REPAY_AMT
				ELSE 0
