	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY			-- 301-330天未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY			-- 331-365天未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY			-- 1-2年未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY			-- 2-3年未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY			-- 3-4年未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY					AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY				-- 4年以上未逾期应收余额
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE									AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,T.before_year_receivables                          AS before_year_receivables                      --查询年前应收
FROM ACDOCA4 T
)
,
ACDOCA6 AS(
SELECT 
	T.COMP_CODE		    			            	AS COMP_CODE                            		-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        	AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            	AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                	AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
	,T.WBS							                	AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        	AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        	AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        	AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        		AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM           			AS LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM           		-- 本币累计余额
	,T.LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AGO         		AS LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AGO  		-- 2023年前本币累计余额
	,T.LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AFTER       		AS LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年新增本币累计余额
	,T.REPAY_AMT	            						AS REPAY_AMT             						-- 回款金额
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES								AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES           				-- 逾期应收
	/*,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_2023_AGO						AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_2023_AGO           		-- 2023年前逾期应收	*/
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AGO	    	AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AGO    	-- 2023年前1-30天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AGO	   		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AGO   	-- 2023年前31-60天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前61-90天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AGO 		-- 2023年前91-120天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前121-150天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前151-180天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前181-210天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前211-240天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前241-275天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前276天-1年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO 	-- 2023年前1-2年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO	-- 2023年前2-3年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO	-- 2023年前3-4年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前4年以上逾期应收
	/*,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_2023_AFTER					AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_2023_AFTER           	-- 2023年后新增逾期应收 */
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AFTER			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年后1-30天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AFTER			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年后31-60天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AFTER			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年后61-90天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后91-120天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后121-150天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后151-180天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后181-210天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后211-240天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后241-275天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后276天-1年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后1-2年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后2-3年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后3-4年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年后4年以上逾期应收
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES							AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES						-- 未逾期应收
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY <= 0 THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1
		ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY				-- 0-30天未逾期应收余额
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY <= 0 THEN 0
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY <= 0 
				THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY
			ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY
			END
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY				-- 31-60天未逾期应收余额
	
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY <= 0 THEN 0
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY <= 0 
				THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY
			ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY
			END
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY				-- 61-90天未逾期应收余额
	
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY <= 0 THEN 0
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY <= 0 
				THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY
			ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY
			END
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY				-- 91-120天未逾期应收余额
	
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY <= 0 THEN 0
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY <= 0 
				THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY
			ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY
			END
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY			-- 121-150天未逾期应收余额
	
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY <= 0 THEN 0
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY <= 0 
				THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY
			ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY
			END
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY			-- 151-180天未逾期应收余额 
	
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY <= 0 THEN 0
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY <= 0 
				THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY
			ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY
			END
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY			-- 181-210天未逾期应收余额 
	
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY <= 0 THEN 0
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY <= 0 
				THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY
			ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY
			END
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY			-- 211-240天未逾期应收余额 
	
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY <= 0 THEN 0
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY <= 0 
				THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY
			ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY
			END
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY			-- 241-270天未逾期应收余额
	
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY <= 0 THEN 0
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY <= 0 
				THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY
			ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY
			END
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY			-- 271-300天未逾期应收余额
	
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY <= 0 THEN 0
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY <= 0 
				THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY
			ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY
			END
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY			-- 301-330天未逾期应收余额
	
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY <= 0 THEN 0
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY <= 0 
				THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY
			ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY
			END
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY			-- 331-365天未逾期应收余额 
	
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY <= 0 THEN 0
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY <= 0 
				THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY
			ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY
			END
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY			-- 1-2年未逾期应收余额
	
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY <= 0 THEN 0
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY <= 0 
				THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY
			ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY
			END
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY			-- 2-3年未逾期应收余额
	
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY <= 0 THEN 0
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY <= 0 
				THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY
			ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY
			END
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY			-- 3-4年未逾期应收余额
	
	,CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY <= 0 THEN 0
		ELSE 
			CASE WHEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY <= 0 
				THEN T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES1 - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY - T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY
			ELSE T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY
			END
	END 												AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY				-- 4年以上未逾期应收余额
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE									AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,T.before_year_receivables                          AS before_year_receivables                      --查询年前应收
FROM ACDOCA5 T
)

INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AR_RECEIVABLE_AGING_2023_INFO_F
SELECT 
	CASE WHEN substr(current_date,1,7) = substr('${PERIOD_ID_D}',1,7) AND SUBSTR('${PERIOD_ID_D}',9,2) IN('01') THEN SUBSTR(last_day(add_months('${PERIOD_ID_D}',-1)),1,10) ELSE '${PERIOD_ID_D}' END 									AS QUERY_DATE									-- 查询日期
	,T.COMP_CODE		    							AS COMP_CODE									-- 公司代码
	,T.CUST_CODE		    				        	AS CUST_CODE                            		-- 客户编码
	,T.CUST_NAME						            	AS CUST_NAME                            		-- 客户全称
    ,CASE WHEN D6.ZVKGRP <>'' THEN D6.ZVKGRP ELSE T2.sales_grp END AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE           -- WBS+客户主档销售组
	, T.WBS                                            	AS WBS                                           -- WBS
	, D6.ZVKGRP                                 	    AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                               -- WBS-销售组
-- 	,T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE		                	AS SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE              		-- 销售组-主档
-- 	,T.WBS							                	AS WBS                                  		-- WBS
-- 	,T.WBS_SALES_GROUP						        	AS WBS_SALES_GROUP                      		-- WBS-销售组
	,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER		    	        	AS SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER               		-- 特别总账
	,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT		    	        	AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT               		-- 总账科目
	,T.CURRENCY		    				        		AS CURRENCY                             		-- 货币
	,T.LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM           			AS LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM           		-- 本币累计余额
	,T.LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AGO         		AS LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AGO  		-- 2023年前本币累计余额
	,T.LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AFTER       		AS LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年新增本币累计余额
	,T.REPAY_AMT	            						AS REPAY_AMT             						-- 回款金额
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES								AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES           				-- 逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO										
														AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_2023_AGO           		-- 2023年前逾期应收	
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AGO
														AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_90_DAY_2023_AGO    	-- 2023年前1-90天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AGO	    	AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AGO    	-- 2023年前1-30天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AGO	   		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AGO   	-- 2023年前31-60天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前61-90天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AGO
														AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_275_DAY_2023_AGO 		-- 2023年前91-275天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AGO 		-- 2023年前91-120天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前121-150天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前151-180天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前181-210天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前211-240天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前241-275天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO
														AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_730_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前276天-2年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前276天-1年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AGO 	-- 2023年前1-2年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO
														AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1460_DAY_2023_AGO	-- 2023年前2-4年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AGO	-- 2023年前2-3年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AGO	-- 2023年前3-4年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AGO		-- 2023年前4年以上逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER
														AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_2023_AFTER           	-- 2023年后新增逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AFTER	
														AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_90_DAY_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年后1-90天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AFTER			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1_30_DAY_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年后1-30天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AFTER			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年后31-60天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AFTER			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年后61-90天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AFTER
														AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_275_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后91-275天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后91-120天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后121-150天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后151-180天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后181-210天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后211-240天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_275_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后241-275天逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AFTER
														AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_730_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后276天-2年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_276_365_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后276天-1年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后1-2年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER + T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER
														AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后2-4年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后2-3年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER		AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY_2023_AFTER	-- 2023年后3-4年逾期应收
	,T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER			AS OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY_2023_AFTER		-- 2023年后4年以上逾期应收
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES							AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES						-- 未逾期应收
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY + T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY + T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY + T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY + T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY + T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY + T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY + T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY + T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY + T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY + T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY + T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY 
														AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_365_DAY				-- 1年内未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY					AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_0_30_DAY				-- 0-30天未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY					AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_31_60_DAY				-- 31-60天未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY					AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_61_90_DAY				-- 61-90天未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY					AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_91_120_DAY				-- 91-120天未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_121_150_DAY			-- 121-150天未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_151_180_DAY			-- 151-180天未逾期应收余额 
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_181_210_DAY			-- 181-210天未逾期应收余额 
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_211_240_DAY			-- 211-240天未逾期应收余额 
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_241_270_DAY			-- 241-270天未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_271_300_DAY			-- 271-300天未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_301_330_DAY			-- 301-330天未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_331_365_DAY			-- 331-365天未逾期应收余额 
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_366_730_DAY			-- 1-2年未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_731_1095_DAY			-- 2-3年未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY				AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1096_1460_DAY			-- 3-4年未逾期应收余额
	,T.N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY					AS N_OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES_1461_DAY				-- 4年以上未逾期应收余额
	,CASE WHEN T.WBS_SALES_GROUP IS NULL OR T.WBS_SALES_GROUP = '' THEN T6.NODE_NAME2
		ELSE T5.NODE_NAME2 
	END                                                AS 	BUSINESS_DEPARTMENT			                -- 事业部
	/*
	,T1.`/BIC/ZA_SYAMB`					                AS BUSINESS_DEPARTMENT			    	        -- 事业部
	*/
	,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()								AS DW_LAST_UPDATE_DATE							-- 数据同步时间
	,T2.cust_grp										AS CUST_GROUP_CODE								-- 客户组
	,'${PERIOD_ID_Y}'                                   AS QUERY_YEAR                                   -- 查询年份
	,T.SO_GROUP_CODE									AS SO_GROUP_CODE								-- 订单组编码
	,T.before_year_receivables                          AS before_year_receivables                      --查询年前应收
	,greatest(T.LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM-T.before_year_receivables,0) AS after_year_receivables       --查询年前应收
	,greatest(T.LOCAL_CURRENCY_BALANCE_SUM-T.OVERDUE_RECEIVABLES,0) AS n_overdue_receivables_result  --未逾期应收
	,CASE WHEN D6.zxsry IS NULL OR D6.zxsry = '' THEN T2.sales_man ELSE D6.zxsry END AS SALES_EMP_CODE  ----销售人员
	,T2.sales_grp                                              AS SALES_GROUP_CUST_FILE              --销售组（客户主档）
FROM 
	ACDOCA6 T
LEFT JOIN DM.DM_RPT_SALES_GROUP_T T5
ON T.WBS_SALES_GROUP = T5.NODE_NAME9
LEFT JOIN DM.DM_RPT_SALES_GROUP_T T6
ON T.SALES_GROUP_MASTER_FILE = T6.NODE_NAME9
/*
LEFT JOIN SDI.SDI_BIC_AZMAM_O0062_1009 T1 
 ON T.COMP_CODE = T1.COMP_CODE				-- 公司代码
 */

LEFT JOIN DWRDIM.DWR_CUST_LATEST_DIMENSION_T T2
ON T.CUST_CODE = T2.CUST_NUM AND SUBSTR(T.COMP_CODE,1,2) = T2.SALES_ORG
LEFT JOIN SDI.SDI_PROJ_1001 D6
		   ON SUBSTR(T.WBS,1,10) = D6.pspid						-- 项目号
;
